**Session summary (2026-08-14, session 116 — staleness sweep fixes: umpires self-heal, WNBA expansion-team injuries via core fallback, UFC phantom-event filter):**
- Started from the scheduled daily staleness check: all CRIT feeds current, but three findings — umpires frozen since 7/12, UFC 8/11 "missing finals" WARN, and site.api.espn.com still blocked for WNBA. Matt: "Can we fix the UFC, WNBA and umpire issues?" Branch `claude/sweet-einstein-do5sjg`.
- **Umpires (the real outage): frozen 2026-07-12 → 2026-08-14 because the daily run moved to 6am ET.** The ingestor's own docstring said it: officials are fetched for `run_date` only and "pipeline runs at 11am so they're available" — but the schedule moved to 6:17am (session 85) then 6:00am on Railway (session 102), and MLB usually hasn't posted HP officials by then. `_fetch_umpires_for_date` fails soft (0 rows, no error), so the table silently froze; coverage was already spotty in late June/early July for the same reason (0-row days whenever no later manual run happened). statsapi connectivity was never the problem (team/pitcher stats on the same host stayed current).
  - **Fix (`umpire_ingestor.py`): new self-healing `run_umpire_ingestor(run_date)`** — fetches run_date PLUS every date in a heal window where an MLB game still has no umpires row. Window = 10 trailing days, extended back to MAX(umpires.game_date)+1 when the table has fallen further behind (UFC csv-loader precedent), capped at 365 days (`_heal_window_lo`, unit-tested). Officials for completed games are always posted, so the table now stays complete with at most a 1-day lag even though the 6am fetch of *today* usually no-ops. `step_umpires` in run_pipeline calls it; CLI default too. **The 33-day gap (7/18–8/13) backfills automatically on the first post-merge daily run** (~28 dates, one schedule call each).
- **WNBA: finals/settlement fully healthy via the session-115 core fallback — but GSV/PDX/TOR injuries were frozen at 8/4** (verified: all 12 legacy teams current through 8/14, the 3 expansion teams stopped the day site.api got blocked). Cause: `_fetch_wnba_espn_team_ids` (team-id resolution) uses the blocked site.api host and falls back to the static `ESPN_WNBA_TEAM_IDS`, which only carries the 12 legacy franchises — the injuries endpoint itself is sports.core and kept working; only the ids were missing.
  - **Fix (`injury_ingestor.py`): `_fetch_wnba_espn_team_ids_core()`** — resolves ids from the sports.core teams collection ($ref chase, http→https forced, injectable fetch for tests), name-joined via the same `_wnba_espn_team_to_abbrev`. Chained in `_espn_team_ids`: site.api first (may recover), ANY site failure → core, both fail → static map (status quo, never worse). Same live-verification caveat as session 115: ESPN is unreachable from the sandbox, so the core teams shape is written from the known core v2 conventions with skip-and-warn parsing.
- **UFC: the 8/11 "missing finals" are a Dana White's Contender Series card — phantom rows that can NEVER score.** Key discovery: ufcstats hosts *profile* pages for DWCS prospects but does not record DWCS bouts (zero Tuesday events in 14.5K fight-log rows), so a fighters-table check alone is insufficient. The Odds API's `mma_mixed_martial_arts` key lists every promotion (session 96b), and phantom rows kept accumulating (Oktagon 8/1, PFL 8/7, DWCS 8/11, LFA 8/14).
  - **Fix (`odds_ingestor.py`): phantom-event filter at ingestion.** `_known_fighter_slugs` = fighters with ≥1 row in `ufc_fight_log` (log-based, NOT profile-based — see above); `_is_known_ufc_matchup` keeps a game when at least one fighter is known (a real UFC debutant keeps its row via the veteran; the scorer's MIN_UFC_FIGHTS gate already skips both-unknown fights, so nothing scoreable is lost). Applied to bulk game+odds rows (`_filter_ufc_phantom` — odds filtered too, else an orphan odds insert would FK-fail) and to the per-event round-totals path BEFORE spending the per-event credit (names alias-normalized via `_normalize_team` so the predicate can't diverge from the game_id path). Fails open on empty slug set / odd game_id shape.
  - **Validated against production:** Aug 2026 replay — drops all 6 DWCS (8/11), 9 Oktagon (8/1), 10 PFL (8/7), 1 LFA (8/14); keeps every fight of the real 8/8, 8/15 (except rasul-magomedov vs sidney-outlaw — both fighters have 0 logged UFC fights, unscoreable regardless), 8/16–8/22 cards; **zero dropped games carry any pick row.** Existing phantom rows were left in place (no destructive GC — they carry odds FK children and age out of the health-check window in ~3 days; the filter stops new ones).
- **Tests:** 3 new files, 19 tests (`test_umpire_ingestor.py` heal-window cases incl. the exact production freeze; `test_ufc_phantom_filter.py`; `test_injury_wnba_core_fallback.py` with dict-backed fake fetch — one test caught a real bug: http→https $ref normalization was inside the default fetch only, moved to the caller loop). Full suite: **22 failed / 390+19 passed — failure set byte-identical to the pre-change tree** (stash-compared), all documented pre-existing scorer/threshold drift.
- **After merge:** first 6am worker run backfills umpires 7/18→8/13 and logs "Umpire ingest: N assignments across ~28 date(s)"; next injuries run logs "ESPN core WNBA: resolved 15 team ids (site.api fallback)" and GSV/PDX/TOR injury rows resume; next odds run logs "UFC: skipped N non-UFC (unknown-fighter) event(s)". The `umpires` health check flips OK the morning after merge; the UFC 8/11 WARN ages out by ~8/15 on its own.

**Session summary (2026-08-11, session 115 — WNBA results ingest: sports.core.api.espn.com fallback for the site.api 403 block):**
- Matt: "fix the WNBA scoring issue with the API." Confirmed the session-112 outage is still live and now a week deep: the `espn_wnba_api` probe shows **`site.api.espn.com` answering the Railway worker with HTTP 403 every day** (IP block), so **23 WNBA games 8/4–8/10 have NULL scores**, `wnba_player_game_log` stops at 8/3, and **6 WNBA BET picks are stuck unsettled** (4 moneyline, 2 assists props). Hard deadline: 8/4 falls out of the 14-day self-heal window ~8/18. The local nba_api job is still down too. Branch `claude/wnba-scoring-apo-0epqo5`.
- **Fix (`data/ingestors/wnba_results_ingestor.py`): per-date fallback to `sports.core.api.espn.com`** — the ESPN host that kept serving the worker through the block (the daily WNBA injuries step reads it; rows landed today). This executes the session-112 documented follow-up ("port to sports.core before the heal window closes"). site.api stays the first try (cheapest, may recover); **ANY** site failure for a date — 403, timeout, HTML-instead-of-JSON — switches that date to the core path, which emits the **exact same record shapes**, so every downstream writer (`_upsert_games_espn`, `build_log_row`, `_upsert_player_log`, team-stats rebuild, settlement) is untouched.
- **Core path mechanics:** events list queried as a **2-day range and filtered client-side by ET date** (`_et_date` via zoneinfo), so an 8pm-ET tip with a next-day UTC timestamp can neither be dropped nor double-counted regardless of whether ESPN buckets `dates=` by ET or UTC. Each event's status/team/score `$ref`s are chased defensively (`_deref` fetches only when the needed key isn't inline; score refs only for completed games). Box scores come from per-competitor `roster` refs (inline ref preferred, **URL constructed as a fallback**) → per-athlete `statistics` refs, with stats resolved **BY NAME with abbreviation fallback** (`_CORE_STAT_KEYS`), never by index; athlete-name docs are cached per run (one fetch per unique player, not per box row). `http://` core refs are forced to https. Box scores are best-effort per team/player — finals are already committed when they run, so a bad roster doc can't roll back a date. ~40 small calls/game at 0.1s spacing; the 7-day backlog is a few minutes one-time.
- **Health probe (`tracking/system_health.py`):** when site.api fails, the probe now also probes the core events endpoint — site-blocked-but-core-OK reports **OK** ("finals land via core") instead of the now-wrong "finals cannot land"; only **both hosts down** reports ERROR. Still WARN severity, still never raises (final_scores keeps CRIT-ing the real impact).
- **Honest caveat:** every ESPN host is unreachable from this sandbox (proxy 403, WebFetch egress-blocked too), so the core payload shapes could NOT be live-verified — they're written from the well-known core v2 schema, documented at the top of the module (DataGolf precedent), and every parse is skip-and-warn rather than crash. If the first post-merge run logs core failures, the shape assumptions block is the one-line fix point. This is the deliberate exception to the session-112 "don't write blind parsers" stance — the deadline (~8/18) and the defensive structure justify it.
- **Verification:** `tests/test_wnba_results_ingestor.py` +5 core tests against a dict-backed fake of the core API (completed game incl. LVA/NYL→LV/NY normalization + constructed-roster-URL fallback; scheduled game's score refs never fetched; the ET/UTC midnight-crossing filter both directions; by-name AND abbreviation-only stat docs; DNP/empty-line skips + athlete-doc caching + `build_log_row` parity) — **16/16 pass** (pytest + direct exec). `py_compile` clean on both touched files.
- **After merge:** the first 6am worker run self-heals the whole 8/4–8/10 backlog automatically (`_target_dates` heal window + trailing-14-day game/prop settle) — no manual SQL. Expect run-log lines "WNBA results 2026-08-0X [core]: N finals" and the `espn_wnba_api` check flipping to "sports.core fallback OK". If ESPN later unblocks site.api, the code reverts to it on its own. The local Basketball Daily Ingest job remains down (NBA's only source — worth reviving before the season, but WNBA no longer depends on it).

**Session summary (2026-08-09, session 114 — losing-models reevaluation: IN-PLAY PROP SCORING BUG found + fixed; batter_runs UNPAUSED; RBI exonerated):**
- Matt: "We need to reevaluate my losing models and unpause the run line one." The game runline (`mlb_runline`) is LIVE (+21.7%/20) and was never paused — "the run line one" resolved to **`mlb_prop_batter_runs`** (batter runs scored), the documented standing unpause candidate. Branch `claude/losing-models-reevaluation-jqxqkc`.
- **HEADLINE BUG — the prop pipeline has been betting IN-PLAY lines with pre-game models since ~2026-06-27.** Found while reevaluating `mlb_prop_batter_rbi` (the only live model showing red: -10.3%/61). Mechanism, three interacting pieces:
  1. The prop scorers had **no started-game guard** — they kept scoring players after first pitch (game scorers skip started games; props never did).
  2. The evening refresh loop ingests DK prop snapshots **after first pitch** as `snapshot_type='open'` (verified: a runs line climbing 0.5 → 1.5 → 2.5 at +600 mid-game; ~28% of all July/Aug DK prop snapshots are post-start). `_get_prop_dk_odds` takes the latest snapshot, so a post-start scoring pass read in-play prices.
  3. **`step_cleanup_picks` (the started-game NONE prune) armed the loop**: deleting a player's pre-game NONE row dropped his key out of the first-signal lock set, so the next 10-minute pass re-scored him against in-play prices — over and over until a churned row landed as BET (locked, settled!) or AVOID (survived cleanup). Pick `created_at` up to 11:32pm ET confirms.
- **Blast radius:** 65 of RBI's 67 settled BETs since 6/27 were created after first pitch (-$1,402 of its -$1,343 total — the ENTIRE "losing record" was in-play artifact). batter_hits 116/119, batter_walks 64/78, pitcher_er 13/48, pitcher_k 11/60, all 5 WNBA props ~100%. Additionally the cleanup **destroyed the graded universe**: every dead-zone NONE row since ~6/26 was deleted before the matview could grade it, so all full-outcome sweeps since July silently saw BET+AVOID rows only (that's also why paused `batter_runs` showed zero cut-clearing picks in July/Aug — its would-be BETs were downgraded to NONE and deleted).
- **Fix 1 (`models/scorer.py`):** new `_game_started(commence_time)` helper (Python ISO parse — 'Z'/offset/naive, never string-compare; None → don't skip) + guard in ALL FOUR prop scorers (batter, pitcher, WNBA, NBA) right after the lock check: a started game's props are never scored again. This is the root-cause fix — in-play snapshots in the odds table become harmless to scoring.
- **Fix 2 (`run_pipeline.py`):** `step_cleanup_picks` RETIRED to a documented no-op (kept for CLI/chain compatibility). The app-side problem it solved is already handled by `fetchPicksForDate`'s `signal_type ASC` ordering under the row cap (signals can never be dropped by NONE volume). NONE rows now persist as the evaluation dataset (~2-3K/day — trivial).
- **Fix 3 (data repair, applied):** flagged **14,023 lock-era prop rows** (game_date ≥ 2026-06-26, created > commence_time+5min, MLB+WNBA props; 401 BETs / 394 settled) as **`is_live = TRUE`** — semantically honest (they WERE in-play picks) and every honest-record surface (mv_scored_pick_outcomes + all 4 record views) already excludes is_live. Matview refreshed. **`created_at` is NOT a valid flag for the pre-lock era** (delete+rescore rewrote rows all evening — May shows "100% in-play created" spuriously), so pre-6/26 rows were left untouched; that era's prop prices may include late-snapshot contamination at lower intensity (10-15% of snapshots were post-start) — unquantified, documented caveat.
- **Clean records after repair (current cuts, view-verified):** `mlb_prop_batter_rbi` **30 bets 11-19 +14.8%** (was -10.3%/61 — KEPT LIVE, no threshold change); batter_walks +26.9%/29; pitcher_k +11.0%/43; wnba_assists +42.1%/13; batter_hits 0 decided at its tight cut (fine). **No live model is losing anymore.**
- **Paused-model re-sweeps (fresh, full grid, -140 floor, min 25 bets):** nothing earned an unpause — pitcher_er best cell -3.8%, pitcher_hits -3.1%, pitcher_outs -3.3%, pitcher_walks -1.6%, batter_tb +2.7%/43 (noise stripe, negative neighbors), batter_sb no volume, WNBA points/threes/rebounds ≤ +1.4% tail cells, pra negative. All stay paused. `mlb_over_under` stays paused — its documented unpause condition (July-inclusive retrain) never ran (active model is still v20260704_104508; the 7/14 Retrain dispatch died on the Actions $0 cap).
- **`mlb_prop_batter_runs` UNPAUSED** at the staged 0.47/0.16 + -140 floor: 40 bets 21-19 **+24.6%**, and the pocket is robust — the whole edge≥0.16 band is +15..+25% across prob 0.45-0.50, plus a second all-positive pocket at prob 0.66-0.72 (0.68 floor = 40 bets 27-13 +20.0%). Monthly: May +3.0u/14, June +6.8u/26 — NOT a single-hot-month artifact (the RBI failure mode). Caveat: all evidence is May-June (July/Aug dead-zone rows were destroyed, see above) — re-sweep after ~40 clean forward picks. Synced all four layers: config (PAUSED_MODELS -1, comments in 3 threshold dicts + MODEL_MIN_ODDS), `model_action_thresholds.paused=false` (applied — app shows it now), mobile thresholds.ts fallback, `docs/mobile_picks_prompt.md`/`docs/thresholds.md` SQL blocks (3 OR-lines restored) + both `docs/thresholds.md` tables.
- **MERGE-TIMING CAVEATS (Matt):** (1) the scorer guard + cleanup retirement only take effect when this branch MERGES (the worker runs master) — until then each evening writes a few more in-play prop rows; re-run the repair UPDATE after merge (SQL in the PR body / this summary's Fix 3 predicate with the date bound removed). (2) A `threshold_sync` run from master before merge would RE-PAUSE batter_runs in the table until merge. (3) **Re-paste the `docs/mobile_picks_prompt.md` prompt into the Claude-mobile project instructions** (batter_runs OR-line restored).
- **Verification:** `py_compile` clean (scorer, run_pipeline, config); record view reproduces the RBI/runs/walks/k numbers above post-repair; batter_runs table row verified 0.47/0.16/-140/unpaused via RETURNING. The 8/9+ evening runs are the live test: expect batter prop picks to lock pre-game only (created_at ≤ commence_time) and zero new is_live prop rows after merge.

**Session summary (2026-08-08, session 113 — custom model builder: filter on side, price, game time, confidence, public and injuries + live preview):**
- Matt (screenshot of the near-empty "New model" screen): "We need to improve the create your own model. Here it should be selecting things like home vs away, favorites model % and edge %. Pick the prob or game time bet. Let them play around with a lot of data points." Mobile-only; no DB migration, no pipeline/scorer/threshold/model changes. Branch `claude/custom-model-builder-yh6n6l`.
- **Starting state:** a custom model was only `name` + a list of `{model_id, min_prob, min_edge}` rules OR'd together — no way to express "road favorites in prime time". The builder screen was a name field, an empty rules card, and a Create button.
- **NEW `mobile/src/lib/customModelFilters.ts`** — model-level filters AND'd over the OR'd rules. Nine data points, all read straight off the `picks` row so **the same predicate runs against today's board and against `fetchSettledPicks` for the backtest** (no games/weather join, so the two can't disagree): signal (BET/AVOID/none), bet type (game vs prop), pick side (home/away/over/under), price (favorite = minus money / underdog = plus money), DK odds floor+ceiling, game time (ET day / early / prime / late), confidence tier, public backing (max/min % on our side), and skip-injury-flagged. Every field is optional and absent means unconstrained, so **models saved before this behave exactly as they did** (`filters` is undefined on them).
- **Ambiguity handled by covering both readings:** "pick the prob or game time bet" could mean a *prop-vs-game* split or a *time-of-day* filter, so both shipped — a Bet type group and a Game time group.
- **`picks.game_time` was in the DB but never selected by the app** (session 59 added + backfilled it; `PICK_COLUMNS` didn't include it). Added to the query and to the `Pick` type — verified **99.86% populated** (104,814 / 104,963 rows since paper start), so time-of-day filtering needs no join. Buckets follow the real ET distribution (day <4p, early 4–7p, prime 7–10p, late 10p+), with **hours 0–4 counted as late** so a 12:41am start belongs to the previous night's tail rather than the next afternoon.
- **Two subtleties worth remembering:** (1) American odds are *monotonic in payout across the sign flip* (−300 < −140 < −110 < +100 < +150 orders identically to their decimals), so the price floor/ceiling is a plain numeric compare — same convention as `config.MODEL_MIN_ODDS`; a comment says so because it looks wrong at first glance. (2) A filter needing a datum the pick lacks **excludes** it rather than silently passing: a price filter drops prob-only picks, and a public filter drops all props/F5 (only full-game ML/spread/totals carry Action Network splits — 2.2% of rows), which the builder's helper text states outright.
- **Builder rebuilt (`ModelEditScreen`)** — chip rows rendered generically from a `CHIP_GROUPS` catalog (adding a data point is a one-line addition), numeric fields for the odds range and public %, an injuries switch, and the model picker now greys out models already added. **The centrepiece is a pinned footer preview**: Today / Backtest picks / Record / Flat ROI recompute live on every toggle, so the builder is a playground rather than a blind form. The draft is matched through the *same* `pickMatchesModel` a saved model uses, so the preview can never disagree with the saved result. New models default to BET-only; an existing model with no filters stays unconstrained rather than being retroactively narrowed.
- `describeFilters` renders short chips ("Home", "Favorites (−)", "DK ≥ -140", "Public ≤ 40%") on the Models list rows and the model detail card. A fully-selected group constrains nothing and is deliberately not summarised.
- **Verification:** new `mobile/scripts/verify_custom_model_filters.ts` — **56/56 pass** (backward compatibility; filters AND rather than replace the rules; ET bucketing incl. the post-midnight wraparound; fav/dog incl. +100 and the no-price case; the odds range across the sign boundary; missing-datum exclusion; toggle/clear round-trips; summary formatting). `npx tsc --noEmit` = **28 errors, byte-identical to the documented `queries.ts` cast baseline, 0 in touched files**. All 13 other verify scripts unchanged — `verify_daily_results` (20) and `verify_sharp_score` (1) fail **identically on master** (confirmed by stashing), pre-existing stale fixtures.
- **One real regression found and fixed in-session:** making `game_time` required on `Pick` broke three existing verify fixtures that build Pick literals — fixed the fixtures rather than weakening the type, since the column is now always selected.
- **Row-cap follow-up (same session, Matt: "how can we solve this rows issue… can it just draw on the overall settled games and keep track separately"):** the custom-model backtest read `fetchSettledPicks` capped at 5,000 rows against 3,186 settled picks growing **28.7/day** — it would have silently truncated around **mid-October 2026**. Matt chose cache + paginate over a server-side rewrite. Three changes:
  - **Paginated** `fetchSettledPicks` (1,000/page). Critically, it advances by `page.length` and stops only on an **empty** page — treating a short page as the end would re-introduce the exact truncation bug if PostgREST's `max-rows` is ever below the page size.
  - **Slimmed to `SETTLED_PICK_COLUMNS`** (19 of ~40) behind a new **`SettledPick`** type. Written as a **mapped type** (`{ [K in SettledPickKey]: Pick[K] }`) not `Pick<Pick, …>`, because the `Pick` interface shadows TypeScript's built-in `Pick<>` utility inside `types/index.ts` — and a mapped type also means adding a column to `Pick` does NOT silently join the cached set while the hand-listed SELECT stays behind. `passesActionFilter` / `buildCalibration` / `computeClvStats` / `HistoryPickRow` widened to structural subsets so a full `Pick` still satisfies them.
  - **NEW `mobile/src/lib/settledPickCache.ts`** — AsyncStorage cache, cached rows render immediately then only a trailing window is refetched. **The window is what makes it correct, not merely fast:** picks don't settle on their game date (`paper_tracker` has trailing-14-day settle windows for game picks, props, UFC and golf), so a cached date can gain or change a result days later. `REFRESH_WINDOW_DAYS = 21` and the server is **authoritative inside the window** — cached rows there are dropped and replaced, never merged, so a deleted or re-graded pick can't linger. First load ≈ 3,186 rows over 4 pages; every load after ≈ 610 (21 days × 29/day) and **constant forever** rather than growing. Cache key is versioned — bump it whenever `SettledPickKey` changes.
- **Model detail now leads with live picks** (Matt: "show picks as they come up"): `ModelDetailScreen` filters `useTodayPicks` (today plus the UFC/golf events scored up to 7 days out) through the same `pickMatchesModel`, rendering a "Live picks" section with game time and DK price above the settled history.
- ~~**Limitation surfaced but NOT fixed:** only BET/AVOID picks ever settle~~ — **FIXED same session, see the every-pick-universe block below** (Matt: "All picks should be settled in 1 database. All of them. Bet, avoid and neutral. Then any model the user sets just looks at all those picks").
- **Verification (cache round):** `verify_custom_model_filters.ts` 69/69 (56 filter + 13 cache: window ≥ settle window, floor at paper start, in-window replacement, deletion handling, sort order). `npx tsc --noEmit` = 27 errors, all the `queries.ts` cast baseline, 0 in touched files. All 19 slim columns verified present on `picks`.

**Session summary (2026-08-08, session 113 continued — custom models now backtest against EVERY scored pick (the graded universe), not just settled BETs):**
- Matt: "All picks should be settled in 1 database. All of them. Bet, avoid and neutral. Then any model the user sets just looks at all those picks to say how well it's doing." He was right that the evidence already existed — the full-outcome views' grading CASE covers every pick and only the final `model_action_thresholds` join was discarding the dead-zone rows. The gradeable universe: **99,482 rows (61k NONE / 35.7k AVOID / 3.7k BET)** vs the ~3.2k settled picks custom models could see before — they were backtesting on **3% of their own evidence**, and any cut looser than the built-in threshold graded to zero.
- **NEW `mv_scored_pick_outcomes` (materialized view, migrations `materialize_scored_pick_outcomes` + predecessors):** every scored MLB+WNBA pick since 2026-04-14 graded from final scores / `player_game_log` / `wnba_player_game_log` — same grading CASEs as `v_model_full_outcome_picks` (incl. the runline away-side sign convention and the O/U honest-era gate) but **no thresholds join**. Carries the filter columns (signal/side/odds/tier/public/injury) plus canonical derived buckets `bet_kind`/`price_side`/`time_slot` — the DB is now the source of truth for the bucket definitions; the client mirrors them only for the live board. **Materialized because the plain view cost 6.2s/query; picks.result stays BET-only** (paper P&L + go-live gate untouched — grading happens on read, the official record is not rewritten). Refreshed by **NEW pipeline Step 0d `step_refresh_outcomes`** (`--step refresh-outcomes`) right after settle — `REFRESH ... CONCURRENTLY` + `ANALYZE`, needs autocommit, non-fatal (a miss = one day stale).
- **NEW RPCs `custom_model_backtest` + `custom_model_picks`** (migration `custom_model_backtest_rpcs`): the phone sends rules+filters JSON (the mobile shapes verbatim) and gets the answer — no 100k-row download. **Performance war story worth remembering:** the SQL-function version took **4.5s** because a filter CTE cross-joined from the opaque jsonb parameter defeated planning; the same query inline was 54ms. Rewritten plpgsql with filters extracted to DECLARE locals → **~43ms**. Also: a fresh matview has no stats — `ANALYZE` after create/refresh or plans wander.
- **Validated:** at each model's current cut the RPC reproduces `v_model_full_outcome_record` **exactly (6/6 models checked)**. The headline unlock: `mlb_moneyline` at a 55% floor = **425 graded picks / −8.99% ROI** (was: zero rows visible); at the built-in 0.72/0.11 = 29 picks 22-7 +26.4%. Filters compose server-side ("home favorites in prime time" = 151 picks −10.9%).
- **Mobile:** `useCustomModelBacktest(model, {withPicks, debounceMs})` — rules on server-graded models (see NEW `isOutcomeGraded()` in customModelFilters.ts, mirrors the matview WHERE) go to the RPC; rules on UFC/NHL/golf (no grading yet) keep the settled-pick cache path; the halves sum exactly since a pick only matches rules for its own model_id. Builder preview now RPC-backed (350ms debounce, content-keyed so identical drafts don't re-fire); ModelDetail "Graded picks" list from `custom_model_picks`; ModelsScreen custom rows via `useCustomModelBacktests`. Pure helpers (`splitRulesByCoverage`/`summaryToStats`/`mergeStats` + stats types) extracted to **NEW `lib/customModelBacktest.ts`** because importing the hooks file pulls react-native into tsx and breaks the verify script. The settled-pick cache (previous block) stays load-bearing for the uncovered-sport fallback + built-in fallback stats + CLV + calibration.
- **Semantics note:** custom-model records will read differently after this — dead-zone and AVOID picks now grade (a BET-only filter keeps roughly the old feel), and pre-07-05 `mlb_over_under` rows are excluded by the honest-era gate where the old settled backtest included them. Both are more honest, not regressions.
- **Verification (final):** `verify_custom_model_filters.ts` **78/78** (+9: coverage split, summary scaling, merge math, empty-half identity). `npx tsc --noEmit` = 27 errors, all the `queries.ts` cast baseline, 0 in touched files. `py_compile` clean on run_pipeline. RPC timing verified via EXPLAIN ANALYZE (43ms); anon GRANTs on matview + RPCs. **Security review DONE post-merge (2026-08-08, same session):** the advisor tool stayed approval-blocked, so its checks were run manually via SQL — RPCs are invoker + pinned search_path (clean), but the matview's ACL showed `anon=arwdDxtm`: **Supabase default privileges grant anon ALL on new public objects, and matviews have no RLS** — anon held MAINTAIN and could have run `REFRESH MATERIALIZED VIEW` with the public API key. Fixed by migration `tighten_mv_scored_pick_outcomes_grants` (REVOKE ALL, GRANT SELECT — verified `anon=r` and the RPC still answers as anon; the repo migration file carries the REVOKE too). **Lesson: after creating any matview, REVOKE ALL then GRANT SELECT — default privileges over-grant and RLS won't save you.** **MAINTENANCE:** the matview is a 3rd copy of the grading CASE — mirror future grading fixes there + extend `isOutcomeGraded()` when a sport gains grading. JS + pipeline change → mobile via OTA after merge; Step 0d activates on the next 6am worker run after merge.

**Session summary (2026-08-07, session 112 — WNBA finals stopped landing: site.api.espn.com refusing the worker; health check made non-silent):**
- Matt: "I don't think the wnba games have been scoring." Confirmed and root-caused. **No WNBA game since 2026-08-04 has a final score** (8/4: 1 game, 8/5: 4, 8/6: 3), `wnba_player_game_log` stops at 8/3, and **5 WNBA BET picks are stuck unsettled** (4 moneyline, 1 assists prop, 8/5–8/6). Clean break — everything through 8/3 scored normally.
- **ROOT CAUSE: `site.api.espn.com` stopped serving the Railway worker on ~2026-08-05.** Not a code change — the last backend commit was 7/30 (`fa2bc64`, feature-engine leak fix); everything since is mobile-only. Nailed down via a clean side-channel: WNBA **injuries** resolve team ids from `site.api.espn.com/.../wnba/teams` with a fallback to the 12 hardcoded `config.ESPN_WNBA_TEAM_IDS` franchises. Injury rows carried the 3 expansion teams (GSV/PDX/TOR) **every day through 8/4, then zero from 8/5 onward** — i.e. the live teams list started failing on exactly the run that first missed finals. Injuries *look* healthy (74 rows today) because they degrade silently to the static map; the results ingestor has **no fallback**, so every date raises → `RuntimeError` → nothing written.
  - The other ESPN host, `sports.core.api.espn.com` (injuries data), **still works** from the worker. One subdomain blocked, the other fine — the signature of ESPN bot-blocking the datacenter IP on the consumer-facing host, though a payload-shape change isn't excluded.
  - **MATT — the exact exception is already in the Railway log**, twice daily: `ESPN WNBA teams list fetch failed (<exc>); using static ESPN_WNBA_TEAM_IDS` (injuries step) and `✗ WNBA results failed: WNBA results: N date(s) failed …` (step 0e). That line (403 vs timeout vs JSON error) decides the fix; I could not obtain it — **all** ESPN hosts are blocked from the dev sandbox (proxy 403, incl. via WebFetch), which is also why I did **not** ship a `sports.core.api.espn.com` fallback: writing a blind `$ref`-walking parser for a feed I cannot inspect is exactly the untested guessing that caused the 2026-07-11 `is_starter` outage.
- **Self-healing is already built and will recover this automatically** — `_target_dates` re-fetches any WNBA game with a NULL score for **14 days** (`HEAL_DAYS`), and the game + prop settle windows are also trailing-14-day. So the moment ESPN answers again, 8/4–8/6 finals, box scores, and the 5 stuck picks all backfill with **no manual SQL**. **Deadline: ~8/18** — after that those dates fall out of the heal window and are lost to the ESPN path (the local nba_api job, dead since ~7/4, is the only other writer).
- **Two silent-failure defects fixed in `tracking/system_health.py`** (this is why it rotted 3 days behind a green run):
  1. **WNBA promoted to `CRIT_FINALS_SPORTS`** — the follow-up the code comment itself asked for ("once the ESPN path has proven itself… consider adding WNBA back"). It did prove itself 7/9–8/3, then broke. Its finals are worker-controlled now, so a gap IS a pipeline failure. `step_wnba_results` logs `✗` but returns False, and a WARN reddens nothing — that combination is what hid this. **Backtested over 7/18–8/7: adds zero false alarms** (WNBA missing-count was 0 every day pre-break) and goes RED on 8/7. The pre-existing MLB/NHL REDs on 7/23/7/24/7/30 are the old rule, unchanged.
  2. **Fixed a circular gate on the `wnba_game_log` check** — it was gated on *finals having landed in the last 3 days*, the very thing that breaks, so on 8/5–8/7 it reported **SKIPPED ("no WNBA finals in last 3 days")** precisely when it should have been screaming. Now gated on games being **scheduled** (cadence-awareness preserved: NBA in July and WNBA off-days still correctly SKIP) and it expects box scores no older than `max(newest final, window start)`, so a stalled finals feed trips it. Verified against live data: today it now returns **STALE** (`log_latest=2026-08-03 expected>=2026-08-04`) instead of SKIPPED.
  3. **NEW `espn_wnba_api` probe** — Matt asked me to check the Railway logs directly; **Railway is blocked by the sandbox egress policy too** (`gateway answered 403 to CONNECT` for `railway.app` / `backboard.railway.app`), no `railway` CLI, no token in env — so a token wouldn't have helped, the host is unroutable from here. Instead of leaving the diagnosis stuck in worker stdout, the health check now probes `site.api.espn.com` once per run and records the outcome into `system_health_checks` (anon-readable), making "why did WNBA results fail" answerable **from Supabase alone**: `HTTP 403` → ESPN IP-block (port to `sports.core.api.espn.com`), `ConnectTimeout`/`ReadTimeout` → transient, `OK, N events` → ESPN is fine and the bug is our parser/shape. WARN + 8s timeout + bare `except` — `final_scores` already CRITs the real impact and a health probe must never hang or redden the pipeline. Gated on WNBA games being scheduled. Verified in-sandbox: it records `ProxyError: …` and returns normally rather than raising.
- **Secondary, self-healing:** GSV/PDX/TOR have had **no injury rows since 8/5** (static-fallback franchises only), so those 3 teams' `home/away_injury_adj` features are stale. Resolves itself when ESPN returns.
- Verification: `py_compile` clean; both new verdicts reproduced against the live DB with the checks' exact SQL; 21-day backtest of the CRIT rule (above). The health check itself next runs at the end of the daily pipeline — expect `final_scores` = **CRIT (red run)** and `wnba_game_log` = STALE until ESPN recovers. **Follow-up if ESPN stays blocked past ~8/16:** port the results ingestor to `sports.core.api.espn.com` (proven reachable) or revive the local Basketball Daily Ingest job before the 14-day heal window closes.

**Session summary (2026-08-02, session 112 — all app filters unified into one shared system):**
- Matt: "Clean up all filters in the app. Make it a better experience for the user look at competitors for inspiration." Mobile-only; no DB/pipeline/scorer/threshold/model changes, no new deps. Branch `claude/app-filters-cleanup-cms9bu`, PR #182 (squash-merged `36708c3`).
- **Audit — four surfaces doing the same job differently:** (1) **Picks stacked TWO bars** — `QuickFilters` (search + Game/Props + sort chips) then `PicksFilterBar` (Filter button + count + Clear + threshold pills) — over a modal with **Cancel/Apply** editing a draft copy; (2) **Stats** had its own Filters button over a **Reset/Done** modal editing live state, containing a **second copy of the sport toggle**; (3) **four** near-identical chip implementations with different padding (QuickFilters chip, PicksFilterBar chip, Stats `chip`, Stats `windowChip`); (4) only the three numeric thresholds ever surfaced as removable pills, so a signal/category/model filter set inside a sheet was **invisible** once closed; (5) category was settable from **two** places on Picks; (6) three words for the same action (Clear / Reset / "Reset all filters"). Up to four rows of controls before the first pick.
- **NEW `mobile/src/components/filters/`** as the single source: `FilterChip` (one chip, `sm`/`md`, optional icon + count — replaces all four copies, plus an exported `chipRowStyle` so every scroller lays out identically), `FilterBar` (search + one Filters entry + removable pills + Clear all + optional quick-chip row via children), `FilterSheet`/`FilterSection` (one sheet chrome: Reset / title / ✕ + sticky **"Show N picks"** footer), `FilterField` (one labeled numeric input).
- **`PicksFilterBar.tsx` → `components/filters/PickFilters.tsx`** (renamed — it is no longer a bar): merges the two Picks bars into one surface. `QuickFilters.tsx` **deleted**. The pure exports `PicksFilterState` / `DEFAULT_FILTER` / `applyFilter` are unchanged (screens depend on them); added `cloneFilter`/`freshFilter` and removed the screen-local `freshDefaultFilter` duplicate.
- **Behaviour change (the one to know): sheets now apply LIVE** instead of Picks' old Cancel/Apply draft commit. Standardized on live because it matches Action Network / OddsJam / Outlier — the footer's result count is the feedback, not a commit step — and it collapses Picks and Stats onto one mental model. Nothing here is destructive enough to need a guard.
- **Every active filter now gets a removable pill** on both screens (signal, category, per-model, thresholds on Picks; team, search, Min GP, min hit %, basis on Stats), with the filtered/total count beside them.
- **Stats:** bespoke modal → shared `FilterSheet`; the **duplicate `SportToggle` inside the modal removed** (it was already in the header); stat chips / window chips / Tonight / team chips / basis pills all → `FilterChip`; **`resetFilters` no longer clears the stat you're looking at** — it resets only sheet-resident filters and leaves the front-page controls (stat, line ruler, window, Hit Rates/Averages) alone, which matches the badge count that already only counted those. Dead code stripped: unused `Modal` import, `BasisPill`, and 26 orphaned style blocks.
- **`SportToggle`:** was **six fixed segments in a non-scrolling row** — the last sports were pushed off the screen edge and unreachable on a phone. Now a horizontal scroller that scrolls the active sport into view on mount, with an optional `available` set (wired on Picks from today's board) that **mutes** sports with no picks — still tappable, since it's the app-wide selector and the empty state explains itself.
- **Verification:** `npx tsc --noEmit` = **28 errors, byte-identical to master's baseline** — verified by diffing sorted output against a stashed tree, not assumed; all 28 are the documented `queries.ts` Supabase casts, **0 in any touched or new file**. 11 of 13 `scripts/verify_*.ts` pass; the 2 failures (`verify_daily_results` 20/45, `verify_sharp_score` 1) **reproduce identically on master** (re-run on a stashed tree) — pre-existing stale fixtures. Device smoke test still pending on Matt's machine. JS-only → ships via the **Mobile OTA update (production)** workflow.
- **CI note (unchanged from sessions 103/111):** GitHub Actions is still blocked by the $0 spending cap — **zero workflow runs were created** for this branch, and every `mobile-preview` run repo-wide since 2026-07-22 dies in ~3s. Absent CI here is expected. August's free minutes reset on their own, but a `$0` budget is a cap rather than consumption, so it stays blocked until Matt raises it.

**Session summary (2026-08-02, session 111 — "no live bets showing" diagnosis + Live tab is picks-only):**
- Matt: "I don't see any live bets showing, is that because none are hitting the model threshold?" then (screenshot) "I don't like it shows the games at the top on live tab. Let's hide those games and just show the picks." Mobile-only; no DB/pipeline/scorer/threshold/model changes. Branch `claude/live-bets-model-threshold-zho7vn`, PR #181.
- **Diagnosis — the live system is healthy; the board is genuinely sparse.** Verified live: 46,118 `live_game_state` snapshots / 8,129 `live_trigger_events` / 7,415 in-play odds rows, all current to the minute — the Railway live loop (session 104) is running fine. 87 live picks all-time, and there WAS a BET on the board at the moment Matt asked (WSH +1.5, p=0.651, edge +15.4%, DK +101). Three compounding reasons it reads as empty: (1) **the Live tab is BET-only** (session 105) and ~78% of live picks are AVOID; (2) **edge, not probability, is the binding constraint** — e.g. a PIT ML at 75.4% model prob still scored AVOID because DK was −760 (88% implied); in-play vig eats the edge; (3) **volume is tiny** — 19 BETs over ~11 days ≈ 1.7/night, and each stands only while its state + odds are fresh (delete+rescore every pass), so a BET can appear and vanish inside a minute. So: yes, thresholds are the main gate, but the 65%/10% cut is doing what it was designed to do — no change made.
- **Live BET record to date: 7-11, −$546 flat** (18 settled, 1 open; runline 0-3, totals 5-6, win prob 2-2). Small sample — `docs/live_betting.md` says treat the first 50 live picks as the calibration set, so thresholds were deliberately left alone.
- **Known blind spot (flagged, not built):** the live scorer writes BET/AVOID only — **no NONE rows** (by design, to avoid hundreds of dead rows per game). So neither Matt nor a future session can see how many sides just missed the cut. Offered a "scored but below threshold" view; not built.
- **Live tab UI (`mobile/src/screens/LiveScreen.tsx`, the only file changed):** removed the active-games banner row that opened the board — it duplicated the matchup already printed on every pick card and pushed the picks below the fold (on the 3-pick slate in the screenshot the second card was half off-screen), and it was rendering with blank scores (`PHI - @ BAL -`). Dropped the banner block, the `activeGames` memo feeding it, the now-unused `LiveGameBanner`/`GameRow`/`EnrichedPick` imports (`EnrichedPick` was already dead), and the `banners` style; subtitle drops the "N active" segment and pluralises the pick count. **No live context lost** — `useLiveGameStates` stays wired and each card still gets `liveState`, so score/inning/outs still render on the card header (session 107).
- **`mobile/src/components/LiveGameBanner.tsx` is now unused** (it had no other importer). Left in place rather than deleted — session 91's precedent was to delete dead files after a removal like this, so it's a standing cleanup candidate.
- **Verification:** `npx tsc --noEmit` = **28 errors, all in `queries.ts`** (the documented Supabase-cast baseline), **0 in the touched file**. Note: `node_modules` had to be installed first — without it the check reports 657 module-resolution errors and is meaningless. Device smoke test pending on Matt's machine. JS-only → ships via the **Mobile OTA update (production)** workflow after merge.
- **CI note (unchanged from session 103):** GitHub Actions is still blocked by the $0 spending cap. Mobile preview produced **zero workflow runs** for this branch — it never fired at all (on 7/22 branches runs were at least created and died in ~3s). Absent CI here is expected, not a fault to chase.

**Session summary (2026-08-01, session 110 — picks show the user's book's line + hand off to that book):**
- Matt (screenshot of a pick card): "It only shows bet with DK. Change that to be whatever sportsbook they select — if they select FanDuel, show the FanDuel line and link to bet with that book. We can also disclose somewhere that our signal picks and build-your-own model go off DK betting lines." Mobile-only follow-on to session 108 (which added the books but still showed/handed off DK everywhere). No DB/pipeline/scorer/threshold changes. Branch `claude/sportsbook-selection-lines-6p2z4w`.
- **What session 108 left half-done:** the preference existed, but the card's price stat was hardcoded `DK <price>`, the user's book was demoted to a small side chip, and the green button was always "Bet on DraftKings" with `pick.dk_bet_link`. The per-book betslip links were already being ingested — verified live: **every priced side at every book carries a link** (props 100% link coverage across all 5 books; game markets same), so the hand-off just wasn't wired.
- **New `lib/markets.displayQuoteForPick(pick, bookRows, book)`** — one place that decides what price/line/link the UI shows: the chosen book's latest snapshot → else the DraftKings price **stored on the pick**, flagged `isFallback`. Returns null rather than inventing a number.
  - **Load-bearing asymmetry: a DraftKings user still sees the STORED scored price, never a fresher snapshot.** That's the number the pick's edge was computed from; showing a moved price beside an unmoved edge would misrepresent the bet. Drift is already surfaced by the movement chip / Line Movement card. So DK users (the default) see byte-identical numbers to before — only non-DK users get live book prices, where there is no stored alternative.
  - **Coverage is uneven and the fallback is the common case, not an edge case** — DK prices 17 prop markets, FanDuel 9, Caesars 8. Verified against the live feed.
- **PickCard:** the price stat is now labeled with the book being quoted (`FD -105`), and **shows that book's own line when it differs from the scored line** (`9 -105` when FD hangs the total on 9 and DK on 8.5) — a better price on a worse number isn't a better bet. The old "yours" chip is replaced by a grey "No FD line — showing DK" note on BET picks (the stat's book label already carries the truth; noting it on dead picks would bury the board in grey text). Line-shop chip suppression now keys off the quoted book.
- **Bet button (card + detail):** hands off to the user's book with that book's betslip link and reads "Bet on FanDuel". Falls back to DraftKings — button text and all — when their book didn't price the side, so the label can never lie about where it's sending you.
- **`lib/draftkings.ts` → `lib/sportsbookLinks.ts`** (deleted + 4 importers repointed): `openBookBetslip(book, link)` with a per-book fallback chain. **DraftKings keeps its verified `dksb://` scheme + store link and its brand green; other books fall back to their website and use the app's tint** — a guessed URL scheme fails silently and an approximated brand hex can fail contrast, so neither is invented. `openBetslip()` remains as the DK-bound alias the parlay hand-off uses.
- **Parlays intentionally stay DraftKings-priced** (legs, EV, correlation, Kelly all key off `dk_odds`) — this was a disclosure ask, not a repricing ask. `ParlayHoldNote` now states every leg is priced at DK.
- **Disclosure in four places:** new Explainer section "Which sportsbook the numbers come from" (signals + parlays are DK-priced; the preference changes only what you see; uneven coverage explained), the Settings "Your sportsbook" card, the parlay hold note, and the All-books footnote.
- **Verification:** `scripts/verify_preferred_book.ts` extended +13 assertions (chosen-book price/link, flagged DK fallback, **DK user sees the scored price not the latest snapshot**, stored-price fallback, null when nothing exists, per-book totals/prop lines, button label) — **41/41 pass**. `npx tsc --noEmit` = **28 errors, byte-identical to the documented `queries.ts` cast baseline, 0 in touched files**. All 7 other verify scripts still pass. Device smoke test pending: Settings → FanDuel, then a pick with an FD line shows `FD -105` + "Bet on FanDuel"; a DK-only prop shows `DK` + the grey no-line note + "Bet on DraftKings"; switching back to DK restores today's numbers exactly.

**Session summary (2026-08-01, session 109 — Stats tab front page rebuilt to mirror the HOF "Leaders" layout):**
- Matt (screenshot of the HOF sports-stats app's Leaders screen): "Change the front page of the stats page to mirror this view." Mobile-only, ONE file (`mobile/src/screens/StatsScreen.tsx`) — no DB/pipeline/threshold/model changes, no new deps, no new queries.
- **The change is where the controls live, not what they compute.** Everything that drove the leaderboard (stat, time window, line, over/under, mode) was buried behind the Filters modal; the front page was a title + subtitle + list. Now the front page reads top-to-bottom the way HOF's does: title/Filters → sport toggle → **stat chips** → **"At Least ▾" + tick ruler** → **"25+ Points" headline** → **window chips (L3 L5 L10 L15 L20 Season + Tonight)** → **Hit Rates | Averages underline tabs** → table. The Filters modal keeps only the secondary cuts (sport, per-game/total basis, Min GP, Min hit %, team, search).
- **Line picker (the ruler).** Replaces the free-text "Line" field. State is now an INTEGER `lineN` + direction, and `lineFor(n, dir)` maps it to the continuous line the hit-rate math already uses: at-least N → `n − 0.5` (value > n−0.5 ⇔ value ≥ n); at-most N → `n + 0.5`. So "1+ Hits" / "6+ Strikeouts" read naturally while `computeHitRate` is untouched. `defaultLineN` = `ceil(stat.defaultLine)` (0.5→1, 5.5→6, 14.5→15), reset on every stat/sport change; ruler max = `max(10, defaultN × 3)`. **`LineRuler` renders a WINDOWED row (offsets −2..+2) rather than a scroll view** — tapping a neighbour re-centres, so the selected value stays pinned mid-row with no width measuring, no `scrollTo`, no momentum-snap math (deliberately chosen over a true scroll-snap ruler, which can't be tuned without a simulator). Out-of-range ticks render as invisible spacers so centring never shifts.
- **Tabs: Hit Rates | Averages.** These are the old `hitRate` / `totals` modes relabelled, NOT a rewrite — Averages keeps the `fetchWindowTotals` path (so the `Season` window and total-vs-per-game ranking both survive) and just defaults `basis` to `perGame` so the tab name is honest. Window chips relabelled L3/L5/L10/… to match HOF.
- **New ODDS column (real data, not decoration).** `useTodayPicks` + the existing `propModelForStat` map today's DK prop price onto each leaderboard row (`player_id` → `{odds, line, side}`, BET rows preferred over dead-zone rows). Column auto-hides when the stat has no prop model or DK doesn't list the market — no column of dashes. Shows the American price + the actual DK line beneath (`o5.5`), so a row can't imply we're pricing the ruler's line when DK's is different.
- **New SPOT column** = the tonight-matchup tier as FAV/NEU/TGH + opponent (the descriptive "vs LAA · S. Gray 5.90 ERA (R)" stays on the sub-line). Deliberately NOT the letter grades HOF shows — `gradeMatchup` produces three tiers, and A-/D+ would imply precision we don't have. Row chevron dropped to buy the width; MLB rows are still tappable. Tonight-only moved from its own row into the window chip strip.
- **Not mirrored (no data):** HOF's Players|Teams tabs (no team leaderboard) and player headshots.
- Badge count now reflects only modal-resident filters (front-page controls are visible, so counting them was noise). Defaults changed: `minGames` 1 → 3, `basis` total → perGame.
- **Verification:** `npx tsc --noEmit` = **28 errors, all in `queries.ts` (the documented Supabase-cast baseline), 0 in StatsScreen**; `npx tsx scripts/verify_hit_rate.ts` ALL PASS (hit-rate lib untouched). Device smoke test pending on Matt's machine (stat chips switch the ruler default; ruler taps re-centre and the headline follows; At Least↔At Most flips the hit column; Averages tab keeps Season; ODDS appears on a stat DK prices today, hidden otherwise). JS-only → ships via the "Mobile OTA update (production)" workflow after merge.

**Session summary (2026-08-01, session 108 — multi-book lines (US top 5) + user-selectable sportsbook):**
- Matt: "Can we add more betting lines. Maybe the top 5 sports books and allow the user to select which one to put on. The model should always use draft kings, but let the user see different lines if they want." Branch `claude/multiple-betting-lines-4btg0t`.
- **Starting state (verified live):** `odds` carried only `draftkings` + `fanduel` (game markets, all sports); `player_prop_odds` was **DraftKings-only** across 17 markets (~86K rows / 3 days). Decisions taken with Matt: books = DK / FanDuel / BetMGM / Caesars / ESPN BET; scope = game markets **and** props; UI = a Settings preference + an All-books table on pick detail.
- **Zero credit cost.** The Odds API counts the `bookmakers` param as ONE region — on the bulk game call *and* the per-event prop calls. Going 2 → 5 books adds no credits. The cost is row volume: props go ~86K → ~430K rows / 3 days. Flagged in `supabase_schema.sql` as a retention watch item.
- **Safety audit first (the load-bearing part).** Verified, not assumed, that every model-facing odds read already pins to DraftKings: `scorer._get_dk_odds` / `_get_prop_dk_odds` / golf / line-movement, `paper_tracker._closing_dk_odds`, and all four game feature engines (which whitelist `('draftkings','sbr_consensus')` — without that, extra books would have **multiplied training rows per game**). `backtester.py`, `golf_feature_engine.py`, `live_game_features.py` have no direct `odds` reads. Conclusion: the change is purely additive and **no scoring / training / settlement code changed**. Both odds tables are append-only (surrogate-PK unique index only), so no upsert changes either.
- **`config.py`:** `LINE_SHOP_BOOKMAKERS` default → the 5 books (still env-overridable). Game-market shopping needed nothing else — `odds_ingestor` already sent `ODDS_API_BOOKMAKERS_PARAM` and looped books.
- **`prop_odds_ingestor.py` (the real work):** props were DK-hardcoded in three places. `_get_event_props` now requests all books and returns `[(book_key, markets), ...]`, ignoring any book we didn't ask for; `_parse_prop_markets` takes a `bookmaker` param (defaulting to DK for back-compat) and is called **once per book** — each call builds a fresh row dict, so two books' prices for the same player/market can never merge. Caller loops books and extends the insert batch.
- **Fail-safe on a bad book key.** This repo has a documented failure where one unsupported request param 422'd and killed an entire fetch (`h2h_3way`, `docs/sports/mlb.md`). Since the sandbox has no `ODDS_API_KEY` I couldn't pre-verify the exact keys, so instead **both ingestors retry with DraftKings alone on a 422** — strictly better than a one-time manual check, because it also protects against a book being renamed later. Caesars is `williamhill_us`, not `caesars` (docs are 403/ambiguous; the mobile label map handles both spellings).
- **New view `v_latest_prop_odds_all_books`** (migration applied; SQL also in `data/migrations/`): prop analog of `v_latest_odds_all_books`, `DISTINCT ON (game, market, player, book)`, excludes `in_play`, `security_invoker` + anon GRANT. Verified as the **anon** role. Reads `game_date` off the table directly — no `games` join needed.
- **Mobile.** Reused the existing foundation rather than rebuilding: `markets.ts` already had `bestPriceForSide` / `lineShopForPick` / a `BOOK_LABELS` map. Added `MODEL_BOOK`, `LINE_SHOP_BOOKS`, `bookName`, `priceForBook`, and `allBookPrices` (sorts by **decimal payout**, not raw American number — `+100` beats `-110`; ties all get the "best" badge). Helper signatures widened from `OddsByBookRow[]` to a new structural `BookPricedRow`, so prop rows (which carry `line` instead of `total_line`) flow through unchanged. Extracted the prop player-name regex out of `PickDetailScreen` into `playerNameFromPickLabel` so the query join and the detail screen share one parser.
- New `usePreferredBook` hook (module-store + AsyncStorage + listeners, copied from `useSportFilter`), defaulting to DraftKings so nothing changes until the user opts in. `fetchPicksForDate` gained a 6th parallel query on the prop view (failure-tolerant, like the other odds queries) and now attaches `bookRows` per pick, game **or** prop. `fetchPickById` does the same for the detail screen. New `AllBooksCard` (book / line / price, best badged, "modeled" + "yours" tags, tap to open that book's betslip) renders after `LineMovementCard`. `PickCard` gained a tint "FD -115" chip for the user's book — exempt from the 2-chip hero cap, and it suppresses the redundant green "Best" chip when they're the same book. Settings gained a "Your sportsbook" card stating plainly that the model always prices against DraftKings.
- **Verification:** 10 new Python tests in `tests/test_multi_book_odds.py` — 3 multi-book parser cases plus **source-level guards asserting the DraftKings filter still exists** in the scorer, paper_tracker, and every feature engine (the tripwire against a future refactor); all pass, as do the 5 existing `test_odds_links.py` tests. New `mobile/scripts/verify_preferred_book.ts` — **27/27** (one assertion I wrote was wrong about which away price paid most; the code was right, the test was fixed). All 8 existing verify scripts still pass. `npx tsc --noEmit` = **28 errors, byte-identical to the master baseline** (the documented `queries.ts` Supabase casts), **0 in touched/new files**. pytest isn't installed in the sandbox, so the Python tests were run by direct exec with stubbed `dotenv`/`psycopg2`/`loguru` — **run `python -m pytest tests/test_multi_book_odds.py -v` on a machine with deps.**
- **CONFIRMED IN PRODUCTION (2026-08-01, post-merge):** all 5 books ingesting on game markets across 4 sports (`espnbet` 3) AND on props — `draftkings / espnbet / betmgm / williamhill_us / fanduel`. **`williamhill_us` is the correct Caesars key** and `espnbet` works, closing the one open question from the build. Model isolation verified empirically, not just by code review: of 44 moneyline picks scored that day, **44/44 matched a DraftKings price and 0 matched only a line-shop book.**
- **FOLLOW-UP SAME SESSION — retention (`data/prune_odds.py`, Step 11b / `--step prune-odds`):** Matt asked whether non-DK rows need storing at all. Measured: `player_prop_odds` was already **455 MB / 970K rows at DK-only**, whole DB **2,053 MB**, and props write ~21 snapshots per proposition per day (2,102 distinct props → 44,878 rows). At 5 books that's ~195K rows/day ≈ 92 MB/day ≈ **2.7 GB/month** — the PR framed this as a "watch item" when it was closer to a real problem. Answer: we DO need non-DK rows (the views read the table; live-fetching would burn credits per screen), but **only the latest per proposition** — verified that the sole non-DK readers are the two `DISTINCT ON` views, and that both mobile history readers (`fetchOddsHistory` / `fetchPropOddsHistory`) hard-code `bookmaker = 'draftkings'`. So ~95% of incoming non-DK volume is unreadable-by-construction. The pruner turns that into a flat working set. Its tier SQL was validated against the live DB before it ever ran (first run will clear ~69K stale FanDuel rows from past games).
- **Bug caught by that validation:** the first draft dated `odds` rows by `game_date`, but **`odds` has no date column** (only `snapshot_at` — which is why `v_latest_odds_all_books` joins `games`). Fixed to join `games`; a test now pins it, because dating by snapshot would prune a future UFC/golf event's only line-shop row (those are priced up to 7 days ahead).
- **Not yet observed at the time of the PR:** the extra books only appear after the next ingest run. Confirm with the two `GROUP BY bookmaker` queries in the plan file, and diff today's `picks` before/after — `dk_odds` and `edge` must be **unchanged**, which is the real proof the models stayed on DraftKings.
